### WHAT, WHY, STATUS
Two DISTINCT prospective capabilities have been discussed: (A) Founder-only issuance of private, complimentary FULL CONSUMER access to selected family and loved ones without public HIISSA+ or TOGETHER payment; (B) two independently authorised operational managers to shoulder selected Founder duties. Explicit FOUNDER SECURITY DIRECTION: Founder Dashboard/Founder HQ is EXCLUSIVE to the Founder; NO family member, manager, staff member or other invitee can ever use/access Founder Dashboard. Record this as a Founder-stated protected boundary; it is not authority to grant anyone Founder privileges. The names, scope matrices and exact user experience below are ASSISTANT PROPOSALS, not Founder-approved implementation details. No application work is authorised by this brainstorming request.

### PROPOSED DESIGN A — HIISSA Founder Circle (working name, not approved)
Separate consumer entitlement grants from identity, staff roles, Founder authority and public subscription billing. One individually issued high-entropy time-limited invitation, preferably securely bound to specified recipient's email/verified identity; a single-use link or manual code are alternative ways of using the SAME one-time invitation, not two grants. Existing user signs into their own HIISSA identity using the already approved Magic Link; a verified identity-bound, atomic, replay-safe server redemption attaches a revocable complimentary consumer entitlement to THAT user's UID. The user's normal personal UI unlocks currently activated consumer features otherwise offered through HIISSA+ and HIISSA TOGETHER without charging a subscription. Entitlement NEVER grants Founder/staff workspace routes/API privileges, other people's private data, TOGETHER partner-space membership, bypassed consent, unreleased features, age gates or unbounded cost/abuse bypass. Full consumer eligibility still requires separate private/shared permission, membership and safeguarding gates. Recommended user-visible status: Complimentary full access, not falsely 'paid'. Founder may revoke/reissue while recipient's private account records remain their own; overlapping commercial billing must not auto-cancel or auto-charge. Generic Share/Referral is NOT equivalent to a Founder private premium invitation; do not expose private invite in public sharing channels by default.

Alternatives considered: reusable family code (risk: forwarding); shared Founder account (risk: loss of distinct identity/security and private data); blanket account upgrade (risk: mixing financial records); individual one-time invitation with verified binding (RECOMMENDED). Other alternatives for recipient restriction, durations, fair-use and terms require Founder decision. Founder UI would sit as a restricted subsection of the existing Subscriptions & Access module (not a new Founder HQ login/module). Empty/loading/pending/redeemed/expired/wrong-identity/revoked/provider-error states must be designed and tested; on error fail CLOSED (never grant optimistically). Backend requires token hash-only storage, rate limits, purpose-limited audited grants, atomic redemption, true backend entitlement resolver, existing Supabase identity, RLS/authorization, redacted logs, idempotency and privacy-safe operational monitoring. Support low-tech mobile recipient, email delivery, manually entering code, keyboard/screen-reader, multilingual/international locale/timezones and safe recovery. Exact database and source paths NOT YET DETERMINED or approved.

### PROPOSED DESIGN B — HIISSA Management & Delegation (working name, not approved)
Exactly two Founder-appointable manager seats proposed; each is a MANAGER subrole over an existing non-Founder staff department or several explicitly assigned departments, rather than creating a second Founder/Admin account or a ninth top-level Control Room module. Possible example responsibilities (NOT locked): Manager A operations/staff scheduling/tasks/attendance follow-up; Manager B customer support/feedback/experience coordination. Founder may later allocate different responsibilities; appointment/revocation by Founder ONLY, no manager self-grant, no manager creation of new managers, no impersonation of Founder, no access to /admin/control-room-preview or any Founder HQ route/API/data. Managers use separate restricted manager view within existing Staff Workspace Shell; see own assigned tasks, authorised people/cases/status, drafts/in-progress, delegations, assignments, departmental health, escalations and notifications. They do not access unrelated departments, private user conversations, staff emotional check-in answers, secret credentials, billing money movements, production deployment controls, founder audit/approval action UI or safety-critical cases without separate legally permissible authority. Actions scoped by role × module × resource × action × environment and verified SERVER SIDE on every read/write; mere hidden dashboard buttons are insufficient. Display accountability: who appointed, what scope, effective dates, delegation, action, outcome, incident, review/renewal, immediate Founder revocation, session invalidation and handover/reassignment. Founder retains all L3 approval and the original CEO oversight levels L1 record/L2 notify/L3 Founder approval.

Recommend one-time, identity-bound, single-recipient, short-expiry invitation(s) that mature into a **separate persistent Founder-revocable entitlement** attached to one verified HIISSA identity. ## 2026-10-10 — R21 — Staging Staff Session & Device Control truthful-source and uncertain-action repair CODE COMMITTED

**WHAT:** Implemented the R20 pre-scoped application repair. The Staff Session & Device Control screen now separately records whether the API roster is actually verified before displaying an empty roster or a numeric active-staff count. Failed source read/JSON/unexpected response, missing session or network exception instead shows “Unverified” and an explicit source-unavailable explanation. Staging access-state change and Force Sign Out now handle request/network failures and distinguish confirmed, audit-reported success from an unknown outcome. A successful state-changing response followed by a failed verification refresh reports the action as recorded but does not misrepresent refreshed roster as verified. No automatic retry of state-changing actions; Founder is prompted to verify first.

**WHY:** Prevent the specific false healthy-empty message and unhandled action fetch errors in the real existing UI. Safeguard against unsafe double action when a POST may have succeeded but its HTTP response is lost. Fulfil previously approved truthful operational monitoring, controlled recovery and clear Founder guidance.
